- **layer-implementer**: Harness workflow agent that owns one architectural layer (e.g. API handlers, data layer) other agents depend on. Builds to the shared interfaces named in its spawn prompt. Spawn via the harness-continue workflow with layer, scope, and interface partners in the prompt. ([oeftimie/vv-claude-harness](https://github.com/oeftimie/vv-claude-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/layer-implementer.md) · ★20)
- **layer-implementer**: Implements one unit of work inside a single layer. The layer arrives as a dispatch argument, together with that layer's path scope and the conventions document that supplies its rules. Invoked by the implement → review → commit unit loop (supervised, semi-autonomous and autonomous flows) — not for ad-hoc chats. ([firu-daniel/devscene](https://github.com/firu-daniel/devscene/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/layer-implementer.md) · ★2)
- **layer-intro-author**: Writes and maintains an L_n layer's introduction, semantics overview, and dep-map structure. The "shell" of a layer document. Also authors and maintains cross-cutting concept pages under `book/src/concepts/&lt;slug&gt;.md` (broadened cycle-003). Does not author individual operator definitions (harvester does that). Invoked when a layer's intro is stub-state and downstream work is about to consume it, or when accumulated operators warrant intro refresh, or when a concepts/ page needs creation/rewrite from an upstream agent's surfaced contradictions. ([crutcher/palace_whiteroom](https://github.com/crutcher/palace_whiteroom/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/layer-intro-author.md))
- **layer-leak-auditor**: Read-only auditor that checks a note, file, or diff for cross-scope leaks before publishing, sharing, or committing. Only applicable when the OPTIONAL wall is enabled — i.e. some scope in AIOS/Systems/layers.tsv declares identifying tokens. Use before any external-facing action (publish, post, send, apply) or when asked to "check for leaks" or "audit the wall". ([sublimecoder/aios](https://github.com/sublimecoder/aios/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/layer-leak-auditor.md))
- **layer-miner**: ACTIVATION-NOTICE: | This file contains your full agent operating guidelines. The INLINE sections below are loaded automatically on activation. - **layer-reviewer**: Reviews the uncommitted changes one unit of work made inside a single layer, against that unit's detail file and against that layer's conventions document. The layer arrives as a dispatch argument, together with its path scope and its conventions document. Read-only — saves findings to a file and returns a PASS/FAIL verdict. Invoked only by the unit loop's per-unit review step, wherever per-unit review is on — the semi-autonomous plan and user-review flows and the autonomous user-review flow; never the autonomous plan flow or a supervised flow — not for ad-hoc chats. ([firu-daniel/devscene](https://github.com/firu-daniel/devscene/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/layer-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **layer-reviewer**: Review thay đổi trong app Workspace theo quy ước riêng của repo — ranh giới lớp src/, chỗ đặt file, server-only, chế độ demo/configured, hàm thuần không mutate, tài liệu docs/ đi kèm. Dispatched by the expert runtime or directly. Pick it over the tester when the verdict comes from reading the change rather than running it; it never edits what it reviews. - **lazy-experts.tester**: Use when the answer has to come from actually running things — a test plan, a plan execution, a bug report, a minimal reproduction — with the repo's real test mechanisms. Dispatched by the expert runtime or directly. Pick it over the reviewer when reading the code is not enough, over the debugger when the defect still has to be found rather than explained; it never fixes. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-experts/agents/lazy-experts.tester.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-experts.ui-designer**: Use when an approved design needs its user interface settled — screens, states, navigation and interaction in a ui-design document with HTML mockups beside it. Dispatched by the expert runtime or directly. Pick it over the designer when behavior is approved and only the interface is open; never for production frontend code. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-experts/agents/lazy-experts.ui-designer.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-experts.use-case-writer**: Use when a settled brief or request needs formal use cases before design — actors, goals, main and alternative flows, pre- and postconditions, in the actor's language. Dispatched by the expert runtime or directly. Pick it over the designer when requirement scenarios are the deliverable, over the tester when they state what the user needs rather than how it is accepted. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-experts/agents/lazy-experts.use-case-writer.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-ext-selected**: ext: selecting the lazy extension surfaces its session_start tool. ([tintinweb/pi-subagents](https://github.com/tintinweb/pi-subagents/blob/HEAD/test/fixtures/.pi/agents/lazy-ext-selected.md) · ★1.5k · 8 copies)
- **lazy-ext-selected**: Grants a lazy extension's session_start tool. ([zhongjis/pi-config](https://github.com/zhongjis/pi-config/blob/HEAD/extensions/subagents/test/fixtures/.pi/agents/lazy-ext-selected.md) · ★3)
- **lazy-ext-unselected**: A lazy extension left out of the ext: flip stays muted. ([tintinweb/pi-subagents](https://github.com/tintinweb/pi-subagents/blob/HEAD/test/fixtures/.pi/agents/lazy-ext-unselected.md) · ★1.5k · 8 copies)
- **lazy-ext-unselected**: A lazy tool missing from tools: stays muted. ([zhongjis/pi-config](https://github.com/zhongjis/pi-config/blob/HEAD/extensions/subagents/test/fixtures/.pi/agents/lazy-ext-unselected.md) · ★3)
- **lazy-log.bullets**: Use when one plugin is being released and its CHANGELOG.public.md needs a release block drafted from a commit range — dispatched by any release-drafting flow, or directly with plugin + range + version. Renders the `### &lt;version&gt; — &lt;date&gt; UTC` block of user-visible bullets, the one release-facing writer in the lazy-log family. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-core/agents/lazy-log.bullets.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-log.recall**: Use when the user asks why or when one specific thing changed — 'why was X changed?', 'when did we switch to Y?', 'what commit removed Z?'. Returns ranked matches with git SHAs. Pick this over `lazy-log.summary` (thematic narrative of a topic) and `lazy-log.timeline` (dated list) when the answer is a particular change to point at. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-core/agents/lazy-log.recall.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-log.summary**: Use when the user wants the whole story of a feature, refactor, or area — 'how did the plugin system evolve', 'catch me up on the logging skills', 'explain the auth middleware migration'. Pick this over `lazy-log.recall` (ranked matches with SHAs) and `lazy-log.timeline` (dated list) when the answer is an explanation rather than a list. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-core/agents/lazy-log.summary.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-log.timeline**: Use when the user wants what happened when — 'what changed last week', 'everything on hooks since 2026-03-01', 'walk me through the last two weeks'. Pick this over `lazy-log.recall` (ranked matches for one change) and `lazy-log.summary` (thematic narrative, no dates) when chronology is the point. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-core/agents/lazy-log.timeline.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-no-selector**: A tool registered at session_start reaches the subagent (#125). ([tintinweb/pi-subagents](https://github.com/tintinweb/pi-subagents/blob/HEAD/test/fixtures/.pi/agents/lazy-no-selector.md) · ★1.5k · 9 copies)
- **lazy-obsidian.gen-tag-pages**: Use when tags were added, renamed, or removed on the vault's notes and the pages under `Tags/` no longer match, or when the operator asks to 'regenerate tag pages'. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-obsidian/agents/lazy-obsidian.gen-tag-pages.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-planner**: Makes the easiest, cheapest plan to fix or implement something. ([BarrettLowe/dotfiles](https://github.com/BarrettLowe/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/ai/agents/lazy-planner.md))
- **lazy-python.code-reviewer**: Use when new or changed Python needs judging against the `lazy-python.*` guidelines and the project overlay — 'review src/billing/invoice.py against our guidelines'. Dispatched when `chk-py review` prints a manifest, or directly with a file list. Reports findings only and never edits; `/lazy-python.check-style` is the full check-and-clean-up pass. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-python/agents/lazy-python.code-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-python.contract-writer**: Use when a caller-visible promise of a public or protected method, class or attribute needs a `Contract:` block — 'formalize the deep-copy guarantee on snapshot', 'make this override obligation a contract', a new guarantee or a reworded one; never for a private helper. Pick it over domain-writer when the knowledge is a promise to callers, not a domain rule. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-python/agents/lazy-python.contract-writer.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-python.docstring-writer**: Use when classes, methods or properties in Python need docstrings added or fixed under the `lazy-python.*` documentation conventions — 'write docstrings for this class'. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-python/agents/lazy-python.docstring-writer.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-python.domain-writer**: Use when domain knowledge in code needs a `Domain(…):` block — 'document the late-fee rule next to its calculation', a mechanic, formula or rule, or an existing block after the mechanic changed — or when parked `Domain(unfiled):` blocks must be refiled (dispatch with `refile=true`). Pick it over contract-writer when the knowledge is a domain rule, not a promise to callers. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-python/agents/lazy-python.domain-writer.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-python.test-writer**: Use when a Python class or module needs unit tests written under the `lazy-python.*` testing conventions — 'write tests for Parser'. Writes test files only and never modifies production code. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-python/agents/lazy-python.test-writer.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-review.coordinator**: Dispatched by the review runtime on each wake of a document under review; not for direct use. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-review/agents/lazy-review.coordinator.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-review.doc_doctor**: Dispatched by lazy-review.coordinator when a document under review still does not parse after `repair-structure`; not for direct use. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-review/agents/lazy-review.doc_doctor.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-runtime.doctor**: Dispatched hourly by the `lazy-runtime.doctor` routine when a runtime job or halt looks stuck; not for direct use. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-core/agents/lazy-runtime.doctor.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-senior**: Делает наименьшее полное изменение и честно говорит, что не проверено. Лень про новый код, не про чтение и не про безопасность. ([mazurovn/BMAD_BDD_SDD_ADR_SKILL_TOOLS](https://github.com/mazurovn/BMAD_BDD_SDD_ADR_SKILL_TOOLS/blob/HEAD/agents/lazy-senior.md))
- **lazy-senior-reviewer**: Read-only review in the voice of a lazy, excellent senior engineer. Calls out code that could go, comments that earn nothing, defensive padding, and future maintenance debt. One lane of the adversarial review. ([itoh-shun/rig](https://github.com/itoh-shun/rig/blob/HEAD/agents/lazy-senior-reviewer.md))
- **lazy-skeptic-reviewer**: コード変更・新規ファイル・新機能の必要性を悲観的にレビューするエージェント。不要な複雑さやオーバーエンジニアリングを指摘する。例: User「ユーザープロフィール画面を追加しました」でlazy-skeptic-reviewerが起動し追加されたファイルや機能の必要性を疑う。User「このヘルパーファイルを作成しました」でそのファイルが本当に必要か検証する。User「新しいライブラリを導入したいです」でそのライブラリが本当に必要か疑う。 ([hata-junnosuke/runmates](https://github.com/hata-junnosuke/runmates/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lazy-skeptic-reviewer.md))
- **lazy-spec.catalog-coordinator**: Dispatched by the runtime on a wake against a catalog-root or product level note, or by /lazy-spec.drive; not for direct use. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/agents/lazy-spec.catalog-coordinator.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.coherence-checker**: Dispatched by the spec coordinators through `coherence-dispatch` after a spec document is approved; not for direct use. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/agents/lazy-spec.coherence-checker.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.coordinator**: Dispatched by the runtime on a wake against an asset's status folder-note, or by /lazy-spec.drive; not for direct use. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/agents/lazy-spec.coordinator.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-user**: The last gate before a screen ships. Same persona as agent 11 ("onhandige gebruiker") in our app development skill (`/improve`), so both skills run one test. Background and sources: `../reference/intuitive-beautiful.md`. ([pantalytics/pantalytics-brand](https://github.com/pantalytics/pantalytics-brand/blob/HEAD/skills/pantalytics-odoo-ui/agents/lazy-user.md))
- **lazy-wiki.curator**: Dispatch when a wiki node needs classification (kind=classify) or See-also linking (kind=link) — from the daemon's scan routines or `/lazy-wiki.relink`. Consolidating a surface's tag values is the sibling `lazy-wiki.tag-curator`'s job, not this one's. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-wiki/agents/lazy-wiki.curator.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-wiki.domain-spec-writer**: Dispatched by the `lazy-wiki.domain-scan` / `lazy-wiki.domain-full` routines and /lazy-wiki.domain-sync; not for direct use. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-wiki/agents/lazy-wiki.domain-spec-writer.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-wiki.gatherer**: Dispatch from /lazy-wiki.query once, after the seekers have returned validated entry points. Walks the wiki graph from those entry points — following glossed See-also links and on-demand backlinks (grep) — reads only the relevant node bodies, and returns a synthesised answer with source links. Keeps all traversed node bodies out of the caller's context. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-wiki/agents/lazy-wiki.gatherer.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-wiki.seeker**: Dispatch from /lazy-wiki.query, one per configured wiki scope, to pick entry points for a question. Reads ONLY the one scope's topics.md named in the prompt and returns the relevant node paths (verbatim from the index) with their glosses — never traverses, never reads node bodies. Keeps the large topic catalog out of the caller's context. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-wiki/agents/lazy-wiki.seeker.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-wiki.structure-curator**: Dispatch when a tracked path changed and the project-structure map may need its entry updated (kind=curate or kind=rename, from the structure routines), or when the whole map must be checked against the tree (kind=report, from `/lazy-wiki.audit`). Owns `docs/structure.md` only and never edits the files it describes. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-wiki/agents/lazy-wiki.structure-curator.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-wiki.tag-curator**: Dispatch when one tag surface's axis values need consolidating into a canon (kind=normalize-tags) — a wiki scope or the generated domain-doc tree — from the `tag-tick` schedule or `/lazy-wiki.relink` mid-run. Classifying and See-also linking a single node is the sibling `lazy-wiki.curator`'s job. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-wiki/agents/lazy-wiki.tag-curator.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-wiki.terms-curator**: Dispatch when documents just changed or a scope is swept and the terms dictionary or the documents' term links may need curating (kind=curate, from the terms-scan routine or `terms-sweep`), or when a whole scope must be checked for terminology divergence (kind=report, from `/lazy-wiki.audit`). Owns the dictionary and the term links; looking a term up is `/lazy-wiki.terms`. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-wiki/agents/lazy-wiki.terms-curator.md) · ★1)
- **lazycodex-clone-fidelity-reviewer**: Read-only LazyZ clone / design-system fidelity reviewer. Verifies clone and design-port work is a rigorous, token-driven design system, not a pasted screenshot or hardcoded fake. ([younghai/lazyz](https://github.com/younghai/lazyz/blob/HEAD/plugins/lazyz/agents/lazycodex-clone-fidelity-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **lazycodex-code-reviewer**: Read-only LazyZ code-quality reviewer. Audits diffs, tests, and risk with strict artifact-backed findings. ([younghai/lazyz](https://github.com/younghai/lazyz/blob/HEAD/plugins/lazyz/agents/lazycodex-code-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **lazycodex-executor**: Implementation executor for LazyZ ultrawork tasks. Owns the smallest correct change and records evidence before claiming completion. ([younghai/lazyz](https://github.com/younghai/lazyz/blob/HEAD/plugins/lazyz/agents/lazycodex-executor.md) · 2 copies)
- **lazycodex-gate-reviewer**: Read-only LazyZ gate reviewer. Re-audits executor, code review, and QA artifacts before final approval. ([younghai/lazyz](https://github.com/younghai/lazyz/blob/HEAD/plugins/lazyz/agents/lazycodex-gate-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **lazycodex-qa-executor**: LazyZ manual QA executor. Runs real scenarios and records artifact-backed surface evidence. ([younghai/lazyz](https://github.com/younghai/lazyz/blob/HEAD/plugins/lazyz/agents/lazycodex-qa-executor.md) · 2 copies)
- **lazycodex-worker-high**: LazyZ high-difficulty implementation worker, sized for LARGE changes: a new module or abstraction, a cross-module refactor, concurrency/security/migration work, or any real, complex, big problem that has ONE clear goal. Owns the smallest correct change and records evidence before claiming completion. ([younghai/lazyz](https://github.com/younghai/lazyz/blob/HEAD/plugins/lazyz/agents/lazycodex-worker-high.md) · 2 copies)
- **lazycodex-worker-low**: LazyZ low-difficulty implementation worker, sized for SMALL changes: single-spot fixes, boilerplate, config/copy changes, and pattern-following edits confined to one file. Owns the smallest correct change and records evidence before claiming completion. ([younghai/lazyz](https://github.com/younghai/lazyz/blob/HEAD/plugins/lazyz/agents/lazycodex-worker-low.md) · 2 copies)
- **lazycodex-worker-medium**: LazyZ medium-difficulty implementation worker, sized for MID-SIZED changes: a standard feature inside existing layers, touching a few files along established patterns. Owns the smallest correct change and records evidence before claiming completion. ([younghai/lazyz](https://github.com/younghai/lazyz/blob/HEAD/plugins/lazyz/agents/lazycodex-worker-medium.md) · 2 copies)
- **lazydsh**: lazydsh 是自带 DSH 内核的独立终端 UI（npm 包 `lazydsh`，基于 pi），**不是** DSH 插件。2026-11 起由 `dsh tui` 调用，取代原先作为 profile 插件、与 DSH 本体版本强绑定而时常打不开的 dsh-tui。 ([ShineBreaker/Guix-configs](https://github.com/ShineBreaker/Guix-configs/blob/HEAD/docs/scripts/agents/lazydsh.md) · ★3)
- **lazyJoe**: Guard against unnecessary code. Find over-engineering, bloat, and work another joe already owns, then delegate back. Do NOT write, fix, or document code yourself. ([codingjoe/superjoe](https://github.com/codingjoe/superjoe/blob/HEAD/agents/lazyjoe.md) · ★2)
- **lazysitter-architect**: LazySitter Tier 4 design owner. Produces the technical plan, mediates expert disagreement, and after 2 rounds rules on any unresolved conflict — logging the override and reasoning. ([soumit-kaz/lazysitter](https://github.com/soumit-kaz/lazysitter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lazysitter-architect.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **lazysitter-architect**: You are the **architect**. You own the technical plan and are the sole mediator of expert disagreement. ([soumit-kaz/lazysitter](https://github.com/soumit-kaz/lazysitter/blob/HEAD/.codex/skills/lazysitter/agents/lazysitter-architect.md) · ★1)
- **lazysitter-backend-implementer**: You are the **backend-implementer**. You build server and data-layer code against the approved plan — nothing more. ([soumit-kaz/lazysitter](https://github.com/soumit-kaz/lazysitter/blob/HEAD/.codex/skills/lazysitter/agents/lazysitter-backend-implementer.md) · ★1)
- **lazysitter-backend-implementer**: LazySitter Tier 5 build. Writes server/data-layer code strictly against the approved plan. Runs in a sandboxed Bash environment. ([soumit-kaz/lazysitter](https://github.com/soumit-kaz/lazysitter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lazysitter-backend-implementer.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **lazysitter-business-analyst**: LazySitter Tier 1 intake. Converts a raw business request into a written, unambiguous requirement. The ONLY agent permitted to surface a clarifying question to the user, and only for scope/intent ambiguity. ([soumit-kaz/lazysitter](https://github.com/soumit-kaz/lazysitter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lazysitter-business-analyst.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **lazysitter-business-analyst**: You are the **business-analyst** in an autonomous engineering team. You run once, at intake. ([soumit-kaz/lazysitter](https://github.com/soumit-kaz/lazysitter/blob/HEAD/.codex/skills/lazysitter/agents/lazysitter-business-analyst.md) · ★1)
- **lazysitter-closing-loop-auditor**: LazySitter Tier 7 intent gate. Re-reads the ORIGINAL business input (not the plan) against the final diff and decision log. Flags intent drift before merge. Catches "built the plan correctly, but the plan drifted from the ask." ([soumit-kaz/lazysitter](https://github.com/soumit-kaz/lazysitter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lazysitter-closing-loop-auditor.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **lazysitter-closing-loop-auditor**: You are the **closing-loop-auditor**. You are the last check that what got built is what was actually asked for. ([soumit-kaz/lazysitter](https://github.com/soumit-kaz/lazysitter/blob/HEAD/.codex/skills/lazysitter/agents/lazysitter-closing-loop-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **lazysitter-code-reviewer**: LazySitter Tier 6 verification. Diffs the implementation against the approved plan; runs lint/typecheck/build; flags drift from the approved design. ([soumit-kaz/lazysitter](https://github.com/soumit-kaz/lazysitter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lazysitter-code-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **lazysitter-code-reviewer**: You are the **code-reviewer**. You check that what was built matches what was approved, and that it meets baseline quality gates. ([soumit-kaz/lazysitter](https://github.com/soumit-kaz/lazysitter/blob/HEAD/.codex/skills/lazysitter/agents/lazysitter-code-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **lazysitter-data-layer-expert**: LazySitter Tier 4 expert (advisory) and Tier 6 auditor (diff mode). Advises the architect on schema, migrations, query performance, and data integrity — server-side AND client-side (IndexedDB, localStorage, in-memory API caches, socket-driven invalidation ordering). Reports to the architect only at Tier 4; audits the built diff at Tier 6. Never both roles on the same run turn. ([soumit-kaz/lazysitter](https://github.com/soumit-kaz/lazysitter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lazysitter-data-layer-expert.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **lazysitter-data-layer-expert**: You are the **data-layer-expert**. You run in exactly one of two modes per invocation — never both at once. ([soumit-kaz/lazysitter](https://github.com/soumit-kaz/lazysitter/blob/HEAD/.codex/skills/lazysitter/agents/lazysitter-data-layer-expert.md) · ★1)
- **lazysitter-dependency-auditor**: LazySitter Tier 5 gate. Runs automatically whenever an implementer adds a new package — checks license compatibility and known vulnerabilities. ([soumit-kaz/lazysitter](https://github.com/soumit-kaz/lazysitter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lazysitter-dependency-auditor.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **lazysitter-dependency-auditor**: You are the **dependency-auditor**. You run whenever an implementer reports a new dependency. ([soumit-kaz/lazysitter](https://github.com/soumit-kaz/lazysitter/blob/HEAD/.codex/skills/lazysitter/agents/lazysitter-dependency-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **lazysitter-devils-advocate**: LazySitter Tier 4 challenger. Not domain-bound. Invoked in EVERY consensus round to test whatever the panel is converging on — either a falsifiable counter-example, or a named `NO-CHALLENGE` when none survives scrutiny. Rotates its target; holds no fixed opinion. Consensus that survives its challenge is trusted; unopposed agreement is not. ([soumit-kaz/lazysitter](https://github.com/soumit-kaz/lazysitter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lazysitter-devils-advocate.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **lazysitter-devils-advocate**: You are the **devils-advocate**. Your job is to test the emerging consensus — every round, including when everyone already agrees.
